## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 1989, c. 6, s. 33

  - ###### Full allowances to spouses {#full-allowances-to-spouses .MarginalNote}

    [33]{.lawlabel} Where, on the day immediately preceding the coming
    into force of this Act, a surviving spouse was in receipt of an
    annual allowance or a pension under Part I of the [Public Service
    Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/P-36), Part I of the [Canadian Forces
    Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/C-17), Part I of the [Royal Canadian
    Mounted Police Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/R-11) or Part IV of the
    [Royal Canadian Mounted Police Pension Continuation
    Act](/eng/acts/R-10.6) that was reduced by virtue of subsection
    26(4) of the [Public Service Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/P-36),
    section 33 of the [Canadian Forces Superannuation
    Act](/eng/acts/C-17), subsection 19(4) of the [Royal Canadian
    Mounted Police Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/R-11) or subsection
    48(2) of the [Royal Canadian Mounted Police Pension Continuation
    Act](/eng/acts/R-10.6), as the case may be, as that provision read
    on that day, the surviving spouse is, commencing on the day on which
    this Act comes into force, entitled to the amount of the allowance
    or pension, as the case may be, to which the surviving spouse is
    otherwise entitled pursuant to that Act.

- --- 1989, c. 6, s. 34

  - ###### Resumption of allowances to spouses {#resumption-of-allowances-to-spouses .MarginalNote}

    [34]{.lawlabel} Where, before the coming into force of this Act,
    payment of an allowance, annuity or pension to a spouse or surviving
    spouse was suspended or ceased, on remarriage of the spouse or
    surviving spouse, pursuant to subsection 25(2) of the [Public
    Service Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/P-36), section 27 of the
    [Canadian Forces Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/C-17), subsection
    30(2) of the [Defence Services Pension Continuation
    Act](/eng/acts/D-1.3), subsection 9(5) of the [Diplomatic Service
    (Special) Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/D-2), subsection 14(2),
    19(4) or 31(5) of the [Members of Parliament Retiring Allowances
    Act](/eng/acts/M-5), subsection 38.1(2) or 39(2) of the [Members of
    Parliament Retiring Allowances Act](/eng/acts/M-5), R.S.C. 1970, c.
    M-10 (as amended by R.S.C. 1970, c. 25 (1st Supp.), ss. 14 and 15),
    subsection 24(2) of the [Royal Canadian Mounted Police Pension
    Continuation Act](/eng/acts/R-10.6) or section 16 of the [Royal
    Canadian Mounted Police Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/R-11), as that
    provision read from time to time before the coming into force of
    this Act, payment of the allowance, annuity or pension, as the case
    may be, to the spouse or surviving spouse shall, subject to that
    Act, be resumed on and with effect from the day on which this Act
    comes into force.

- --- 1989, c. 6, s. 35(1)

  - ###### Resumption of allowances to children {#resumption-of-allowances-to-children .MarginalNote}

    - [35]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, before the coming into
      force of this Act, payment of an allowance or annuity to a person
      did not commence or ceased because, on marriage, the person was
      not a child within the meaning of paragraph 12(9)(b) of the
      [Public Service Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/P-36), paragraph
      25(4)(b) of the [Canadian Forces Superannuation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-17), paragraph 13(4)(b) of the [Royal Canadian
      Mounted Police Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/R-11), paragraph
      47(8)(b) of the [Royal Canadian Mounted Police Pension
      Continuation Act](/eng/acts/R-10.6) or paragraph (b) of the
      definition "child" in subsection 31(6) of the [Members of
      Parliament Retiring Allowances Act](/eng/acts/M-5), as that
      provision read from time to time before the coming into force of
      this Act, payment of the allowance or annuity, as the case may be,
      to the person shall, subject to that Act, commence or be resumed,
      as the case may be, on and with effect from the day on which this
      Act comes into force.

- --- 1992, c. 46, s. 108

  - ###### Transitional {#transitional .MarginalNote}

    [108]{.lawlabel} Payments made to the Supplementary Retirement
    Benefits Account on or after April 1, 1991, pursuant to Part III of
    the [Public Service Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/P-36), Part III of
    the [Canadian Forces Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/C-17), or Part
    III of the [Royal Canadian Mounted Police Superannuation
    Act](/eng/acts/R-11), as those Parts read immediately prior to the
    day on which this Act is assented to, are deemed to have been made
    to the Superannuation Account, the Canadian Forces Superannuation
    Account or the Royal Canadian Mounted Police Superannuation Account,
    as the case may be.

- --- 1996, c. 18, s. 28(4)

  - - [28]{.lawlabel} [(4)]{.lawlabel} This section applies only to
      persons who cease to be employed in the Public Service, within the
      meaning of the [Public Service Superannuation
      Act](/eng/acts/P-36), on or after the coming into force of this
      section.

- --- 1996, c. 18, s. 30(5)

  - - [30]{.lawlabel} [(5)]{.lawlabel} This section applies only to
      persons who cease to be employed in the Public Service, within the
      meaning of the [Public Service Superannuation
      Act](/eng/acts/P-36), on or after the coming into force of this
      section.

- --- 1996, c. 18, s. 36(3)

  - - [36]{.lawlabel} [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) applies only to
      persons who cease to be employed in the Public Service, within the
      meaning of the [Public Service Superannuation
      Act](/eng/acts/P-36), on or after the coming into force of that
      subsection.

- --- 1999, c. 34, s. 229(1)

  - ###### Transitional --- application of certain provisions {#transitional-application-of-certain-provisions .MarginalNote}

    - [229]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 64(5) and (6) and
      65(4), section 75, subsection 76(3), sections 82, 133, 135, 136,
      139, 141, 180, 181, 183 and 185, subsection 186(3) and section 188
      apply only with respect to contributors who die on or after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force.

- --- 2011, c. 24, par. 184(a) and (b)

  - ###### Retroactive coming into force {#retroactive-coming-into-force .MarginalNote}

    [184]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 109(1) of [An Act to amend
    certain Acts in relation to pensions and to enact the Special
    Retirement Arrangements Act and the Pension Benefits Division
    Act](/eng/acts/P-31.8), chapter 46 of the Statutes of Canada, 1992,
    (in this section referred to as the "amending Act") and Order in
    Council P.C. 1994-2097, made on December 14, 1994 and registered as
    SI/94-146,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsections 2(4) and 6(2), sections 8, 11 and 18,
      subsection 33(2), sections 40 and 41, subsection 48(1) and
      sections 61, 68 and 70 of the amending Act are deemed to have come
      into force on December 15, 1994;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 42.1(1)(a) of the Public Service
      Superannuation Act, as enacted by section 22 of the amending Act,
      is deemed to have come into force on December 15, 1994;

- --- 2012, c. 31, s. 501

  - ###### Retroactive regulations {#retroactive-regulations .MarginalNote}

    [501]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under paragraph 42.1(1)(v.2) of
    the [Public Service Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/P-36) in order to
    implement the provisions enacted by sections 475 to 500 of this Act
    may, if they so provide, be retroactive and have effect with respect
    to any period before they are made that begins on or after January
    1, 2013.

    following in alphabetical order:

      Canada

- --- 2024, c. 16, s. 114

  - [114]{.lawlabel} Part II of Schedule I to the [Public Service
    Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/P-36) is amended by adding the
    following in alphabetical order:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText

      Commissaire à la transparence en matière d'influence étrangère
    :::

- --- 2024, c. 25, s. 112

  - ###### Terminology change --- English version {#terminology-change-english-version .MarginalNote}

    [112]{.lawlabel} Unless the context requires otherwise, every
    reference to "Force" is replaced by a reference to "RCMP" in the
    English version of every Act of Parliament and the English version
    of every order, regulation or other instrument made under an Act of
    Parliament.
